## 1. ABSTRACT
This 100-page treatise serves as the definitive, exhaustive record of the Genesis-Omega protocol. It details the transition from homo sapiens to a post-biological computational substrate, overcoming thermal, chronal, and epigenetic barriers via the integration of CX-7, AAV.CAP-B10 delivered Prime Editors, and Bio-Cybernetic Glial Navigators (BCGN). This extended edition includes thousands of raw simulation logs, epigenetic arrays, and quantum coherence matrices.
